Seeded with every team owning its own picks for the next three drafts (C5: 2037–2039 from 2036; 672 rows); seeding never overwrites an existing row. JR corrects a CPU-era pick trade in the `#debug` table editor.
- **`gm_trade_proposals`**: `id`, `season`, `week` (`league_state.current_week` when made), `trade_window` (`IN_SEASON` | `OFFSEASON` | `DRAFT_DAY`; the plan's `window`, renamed because WINDOW is reserved), `trigger_kind` (`NEED` | `INJURY` | `SELL` | `DRAFT` | `USER`; the plan's `trigger`), `origin` (`ENGINE` | `USER`: a trade JR recorded or applied from a sighting), `expires_week`, `team_a_id` (initiator), `team_b_id`, `value_a` / `value_b` (each side's surplus Δ, dollars), `league_value_a` / `league_value_b` (neutral value each side receives), `cap_space_before_a/b`, `cap_space_after_a/b`, `dead_money_a/b`, `roster_after_a/b`, `cap_derived_a/b`, `legality` JSONB (`{check: {ok, detail}}`), `rationale_a`, `rationale_b`, `headline`, `rationale_source` (`LLM` | `TEMPLATE`), `status` (`PROPOSED` → `APPROVED` → `EXECUTED` | `REJECTED` | `EXPIRED`), `status_reason`, `approved_at`, `executed_at`, `executed_as` (`PROPOSED` | `JR_EDITED` | `SIGHTING`), `rumor_ok` (step 5 may use a non-executed proposal as a rumor), `flags` TEXT[], `deal_hash`, `created_at`. `UNIQUE (season, deal_hash)`, index `(season, status)`.
- **`gm_trade_assets`**: `id`, `proposal_id` (FK CASCADE), `from_team_id`, `to_team_id`, `asset_type` (`PLAYER` | `PICK`), `player_id`, `contract_id`, `pick_draft_season`, `pick_round`, `pick_original_team_id`, `cap_hit_now`, `value_from`, `value_to`, `value_league`.
- **`gm_trade_sightings`**: trade rows the League Transactions scan read (the loader never applies them): `id`, `season`, `txn_date`, `raw_text`, `parsed` JSONB, `capture_id`, `proposal_id` (FK SET NULL), `match_status` (`MATCHED` | `PARTIAL` | `UNMATCHED`), `created_at`; `UNIQUE (season, txn_date, raw_text)`.
- FK `transactions.gm_proposal_id → gm_trade_proposals` (`transactions_gm_proposal_fk`, ON DELETE SET NULL) and `draft_picks.acquired_proposal_id → gm_trade_proposals` (`draft_picks_acquired_proposal_fk`).

### 3.4 Fantasy Analytics & Projections
- **`fantasy_projections`**:
  - `id` (PK), `season` (int), `week` (int), `player_id` (int, players.id — no FK constraint), `player_name` (varchar), `position` (varchar), `team_id` (FK, int), `projected_points` (numeric), `avg_points` (numeric), `matchup_stars` (int 1–5), `pos_rank` (varchar e.g. 'QB1', 'WR1'), `adp_rank` (int), `ros_rank` (varchar), `ros_total_points` (numeric), `delta` (numeric). `UNIQUE(season, week, player_id)`.
  - **Week 1 = preseason ADP board & baseline projections**: `engines/fantasy_adp_engine.py` seeds the top 300 players by simulated ADP (`fantasy_sim_adp.adp`, ties by `draft_score`), setting `adp_rank` = that order. Preseason calibration by `engines/fantasy_projection_engine.py` then projects Week 1 and rest-of-season for all rostered QB/HB/FB/WR/TE/K/DEF:
    - Preseason baseline weights the player's prior-season stats shrunk toward the depth-chart prior by `PRESEASON_PRIOR_GAMES = 4.0`: `baseline = (total_prev + 4.0 * slot_prior) / (games_prev + 4.0)`. `avg_points` reflects prior-season per-game average; `delta` = `projected_points − avg_points`.
    - For rookies or players without prior-season lines, baseline uses the depth-chart slot prior scaled by player overall rating: `slot_prior * max(0.6, min(1.4, 1.0 + (ovr - 80) * 0.015))`, with `avg_points` and `delta` NULL. Slot priors come from the prior season; with no prior-season games they fall back to `DEFAULT_SLOT_PRIORS` (e.g. QB1 17.0, RB1 12.0, WR1 16.0, TE1 8.5, K 7.5, DEF 6.5).
    - Prior-season K/DEF lines use the same kicking/defense scoring as in-season (below). Only 29 of 285 completed 2035 games have `game_stat_defense` lines (and 71 kicking lines), so 2036 week-1 DEF projections are mostly the points-allowed tier (≈0.4 average); seasons captured weekly have full lines.
    - Preseason matchup factors and `matchup_stars` use the prior season's defensive allowed-per-game per position group shrunk toward the league average.
    - Active injuries (`injuries` row with `weeks_remaining > 0` or `is_ir = true`) zero out `projected_points`, omit the player from `pos_rank`, and exclude injured weeks from `ros_total_points`.
    - Every playing, non-injured player receives `pos_rank` (`QB1`, `RB1`, etc.), and every rostered player receives `ros_total_points` (sum of projections across weeks 1–18) and `ros_rank`. Preseason `adp_rank` is preserved on the week 1 rows so `rank_trend` (`adp_rank − overall_ros_rank`) works across the board.
  - **Weeks 2–18 = in-season weekly projections**, written by `engines/fantasy_projection_engine.py`: one row per rostered QB/HB/FB/WR/TE, kicker (K) and team defense (DEF, one `player_seasons` row per team), using only games completed before that week, so past weeks show what was projected before kickoff. Re-running a week replaces its rows (rows for players no longer projected are deleted).
    - **Points per game line** (`load_points_rows`): skill players from `player_stats.fantasy_points` (positions QB/HB/FB/WR/TE only, so K/DEF are never double-counted); kickers from `game_stat_kicking`: `3·FGM + FGM_40–49 + 2·FGM_50+ + XPM` (40–49 yd FG = 4, 50+ = 5); DEF from `game_stat_defense` summed per team and game: `sacks + 2·INT + 2·fumble recoveries + 6·TD + 2·safeties + 2·blocks` plus a points-allowed tier (0 → 10, ≤6 → 7, ≤13 → 4, ≤20 → 1, ≤27 → 0, ≤34 → −1, more → −4). The K/DEF SQL lives in `KICKING_POINTS_SQL` / `DEF_POINTS_SQL` / `DEF_STATS_JOIN_SQL` and is reused by Napper's actual-score CTE (`server/napper_api.py`), so projections and Napper scores agree.
    - `projected_points` = baseline × matchup factor. Baseline is the player's PPR per team game to date (team games with no stat line count as 0; the weeks are his current team's completed weeks plus every week he has a line, so a traded player keeps his old-team games and points, League Transactions §3.3e), shrunk toward a depth-chart prior by 0.25 pseudo-games; the prior is the league's PPR per team game for that depth slot (rank by OVR within team and group, capped at QB2/RB3/WR4/TE2/K1/DEF1). Matchup factor = (opponent's PPR allowed to the group, shrunk toward the league average by 16 pseudo-games, ÷ league average)^0.5; because D/ST points can be negative, the ratio is floored at `MIN_MATCHUP_RATIO = 0.25` and a league average ≤ 0 gives a neutral 1.0.
    - `avg_points` = PPR per appearance to date; `delta` = `projected_points − avg_points`.
    - `matchup_stars` = the opponent's PPR allowed per game to the group, split into five even groups (Postgres-style `NTILE(5)`) across the 32 defenses, 1 = toughest, 5 = easiest. Point-in-time and per game, so it can differ from `vw_fantasy_defense_vs_position` (season totals).
    - `pos_rank` ranks players with a game that week within QB/RB/WR/TE/K/DEF (RB = HB + FB), e.g. `RB12`, `K3`, `DEF7`. Players on bye get `projected_points` 0, NULL `pos_rank`/`matchup_stars`/`delta`, so they stay on the ROS board but drop out of that week's rankings. Active injuries (`weeks_remaining > 0` or `is_ir = true` in `injuries`) similarly zero out `projected_points` and set NULL `pos_rank`/`matchup_stars`/`delta`, with injured weeks excluded from `ros_total_points`.
    - `ros_total_points` = sum of the player's projections for that week through week 18 against each remaining opponent; `ros_rank` ranks it within the group.
    - **Released players** (`player_seasons.released_week` ≤ the week, §3.3e) have no team game: `projected_points` 0 like a bye, no depth slot (they don't shift the depth-chart priors), and no ROS weeks from the release on.
    - `adp_rank` is copied from the season's week 1 row so ROS `TREND` (`adp_rank − overall_ros_rank`) works.
    - **Accuracy (2035 backtest, weeks 2–17, depth-chart starters QB1/RB1–2/WR1–3/TE1):** MAE 3.29 PPR vs. 6.18 for guessing the league average; correlation 0.79. Almost all of it comes from the per-game average. Production here is very stable week to week; the matchup factor adds little, and recency weighting was tested and dropped. Check with `--backtest` (now includes K/DEF: 4,608 starter-weeks, MAE 3.25, correlation 0.805). K/DEF on their own are noisy: on 2036 weeks 2–4 they trail a league-average guess (K MAE 4.87 vs 4.13, DEF 5.23 vs 3.87), as expected with two or three games of data.
  - When a player has no stored row for the week, the player outlook (`GET /api/player/{id}`, §4.3) falls back to a rough estimate with no ranks.
- **Simulated fantasy leagues** (`engines/fantasy_league_sim.py`, `db/migrations/2026_10_21_fantasy_league_sim.sql`; design in `docs/FANTASY_EDITORIAL_MATRIX.md` §5). 100 leagues × 12 teams, full PPR, 15 rounds, lineup QB/2 RB/2 WR/TE/FLEX (skill players only: `load_week_data` drops the K/DEF rows that `load_season` now returns):
  - **Draft** (once per season, from `fantasy_adp_engine`; stored, so in-season OVR changes don't redraft): snake order. Each manager reads `draft_score` with personal noise (sd 1.2%) and values players over replacement (the 12th QB/TE, 30th RB/WR; QB value × 0.4, TE × 0.8), minus backup penalties (6 per QB, 10 per TE already rostered; RB/WR past five fade) and caps (QB/TE 2, RB/WR 7); late picks are forced to fill an empty starting slot. Strategy adjustments: `BPA` none; `HERO_RB` one RB in rounds 1–2 then none through round 5; `ZERO_RB` no RB before round 5; `RB_HEAVY` 2–3 RBs in rounds 1–4; `EARLY_QB` / `EARLY_TE` one in rounds 1–3. Seats draw strategies independently from the season mix.
  - **Strategy mix:** first season `DEFAULT_MIX` (BPA 35%, Hero RB 15%, Zero RB 15%, RB Heavy 15%, Early QB 10%, Early TE 10%). Later seasons: lift = title share ÷ manager share; new ∝ old × lift^0.5, blended 50/50 with the old mix, floor 5%.
  - **Season** (re-simulated from the stored draft on every run, seeded per season/league/week): lineups start the highest `projected_points` (week 1: best ADP); scores are actual PPR (`player_stats.fantasy_points`); 12-team round robin weeks 1–14; seeds by wins then points for; 6-team playoffs (seeds 1–2 bye in week 15, semifinals week 16, final week 17; ties to the higher seed). Before each week, managers in reverse-standings order make up to 2 add/drops (each with probability = `activity`, 0.2–1.0) when a free agent's value beats the drop's by 15% + 1 point: value = `ros_total_points` scaled to the fantasy weeks left (playoff weeks: next game × weeks left; `ros_base`), × (1 + `reactivity` (0–0.5) × last week's PPR vs. his average, clamped −0.5..1; `hot_signal`). Napper's waiver run reuses `ros_base` and `hot_signal` and the same manager traits (§4.4b). They also stream a QB/RB/WR/TE when no rostered player at a starting position plays that week.
  - **`fantasy_sim_picks`**: `season`, `league`, `pick` (1–180), `round`, `team` (1–12 = draft slot), `player_id`. PK `(season, league, pick)`.
  - **`fantasy_sim_adp`**: `season`, `player_id`, `adp` (mean pick over 100 drafts; undrafted = pick 181), `min_pick`/`max_pick` (drafted leagues only; NULL if never drafted), `draft_pct`. Every board player (QB/HB/WR/TE) has a row.
  - **`fantasy_sim_player_weeks`**: `season`, `week` (rosters/lineups going into that week), `player_id`, `rostered_pct`, `started_pct` (NULL for a week without projections). Only players on at least one roster. Rewritten on every run.
  - **`fantasy_sim_teams`**: per league seat: `strategy`, `activity`, `reactivity`, `wins`/`losses`/`ties`, `points_for`/`points_against`, `seed` (after week 14), `playoff_finish` (`CHAMPION`, `RUNNER_UP`, `SEMIFINAL`, `QUARTERFINAL`, `MISSED`), `final_rank` (1–12 once week 17 is final).
  - **`fantasy_sim_strategy_mix`**: per season and strategy: `share` (target mix), `managers` (seats drawn), and once week 17 is final `titles`, `playoff_teams`, `avg_points_for` (regular season). Feeds the next season's mix; not published in articles.
  - 2035 (first run, drafted from end-of-season ratings): ADP 1 Trey'Mane Davis 1.8 (range 1–6); titles BPA 33, Zero RB 24, Early QB 20, Hero RB 14, RB Heavy 8, Early TE 1. A full season simulates in about 3 seconds.
- **`vw_fantasy_scores` (View)**:
  - `game_id`, `player_name`, `team_id`, `season`, `week`, `ppr_score` (= `player_stats.fantasy_points`).
- **`vw_fantasy_defense_vs_position` (View)**:
  - Aggregates PPR fantasy points conceded by each defensive team per position across completed games.
  - Dynamically calculates:
    - `total_allowed`, `avg_allowed`
    - `min_allowed`, `max_allowed`, `league_avg_allowed`
    - `matchup_stars`: `NTILE(5) OVER (PARTITION BY season, position ORDER BY total_allowed ASC)::numeric`.

### 3.4b Napper Interactive Fantasy Platform (`db/migrations/2026_12_02_napper_fantasy.sql`)
Multi-league interactive Sleeper-style fantasy ecosystem supporting real-time matchups, AI trade negotiations, free agency waivers, and autonomous manager chat banter:
- **`napper_user_profiles`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `handle` (varchar(32) UNIQUE, e.g. `@jrereyi2000`), `display_name` (varchar(64)), `avatar_url` (text), `trophies` (int DEFAULT 0), `created_at` (timestamptz).
- **`napper_leagues`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `name` (varchar(64)), `season` (int; Napper-created leagues use the active `league_state.current_season`), `teams_count` (smallint DEFAULT 8), `scoring_type` (varchar(16) DEFAULT 'PPR'), `roster_format` (jsonb DEFAULT standard 1-QB slots: `{"QB": 1, "RB": 2, "WR": 2, "TE": 1, "FLEX": 1, "SUPERFLEX": 0, "K": 1, "DEF": 1, "BN": 6, "IR": 1}`; Superflex is included only when configured; `IR` = injured-reserve slots, added to every league by `2026_12_04_napper_ir_slot.sql`), `current_week` (int; new in-season leagues use `league_state.current_week`), `status` (varchar(16) DEFAULT 'IN_SEASON'), `avatar_url` (text), `is_seed` (boolean DEFAULT FALSE; seed leagues are excluded from the active league list), `champion_team_id` (FK `napper_teams(id)` ON DELETE SET NULL; set when the week 17 final is decided, `2026_12_06_napper_playoffs.sql`).
- **`napper_teams`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `league_id` (FK `napper_leagues(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `user_id` (FK `napper_user_profiles(id)` ON DELETE SET NULL, NULL for AI teams), `team_name` (varchar(64)), `manager_handle` (varchar(32)), `avatar_url` (text), `is_ai` (boolean DEFAULT FALSE), `persona` (varchar(32), e.g. `USER`, `TRASH_TALKER`, `ANALYTICS_NERD`, `WAIVER_HAWK`, `DIE_HARD`, `ZERO_RB_ZEALOT`, `HERO_RB`, `TRADE_FIEND`, `UNDERDOG`), `wins` (int DEFAULT 0), `losses` (int DEFAULT 0), `ties` (int DEFAULT 0), `points_for` (numeric(8,2) DEFAULT 0), `points_against` (numeric(8,2) DEFAULT 0), `seed` (smallint).
- **`napper_rosters`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `league_id` (FK `napper_leagues(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `team_id` (FK `napper_teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `week` (int NOT NULL), `player_id` (FK `players(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `slot` (varchar(16) NOT NULL, e.g. `QB`, `RB`, `WR`, `TE`, `FLEX`, `SUPERFLEX`, `K`, `DEF`, `BN`, `IR`). `BN` and `IR` don't start or score; `IR` players are also left out of trades and of the matchup view. Unique constraint: `UNIQUE(team_id, week, player_id)`.
- **`napper_matchups`**:
  - `playoff_round` (varchar(16), `2026_12_06_napper_playoffs.sql`): `QUARTERFINAL` (week 15), `SEMIFINAL` (16), `FINAL` (17), NULL in the regular season. Playoff-week rows are written only by `engines/napper_playoffs.sync_playoffs`; teams outside the bracket have no matchup in weeks 15–17.
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `league_id` (FK `napper_leagues(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `week` (int NOT NULL), `home_team_id` (FK `napper_teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `away_team_id` (FK `napper_teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `home_score` (numeric(6,2) DEFAULT 0), `away_score` (numeric(6,2) DEFAULT 0), `home_proj` (numeric(6,2) DEFAULT 0), `away_proj` (numeric(6,2) DEFAULT 0), `win_prob_home` (numeric(4,3) DEFAULT 0.500), `is_final` (boolean DEFAULT FALSE).
- **`napper_trades`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `league_id` (FK `napper_leagues(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `week` (int NOT NULL), `proposer_team_id` (FK `napper_teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `target_team_id` (FK `napper_teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `offered_player_ids` (int[]), `requested_player_ids` (int[]), `status` (varchar(16) DEFAULT 'PENDING' — `PENDING`, `ACCEPTED`, `REJECTED`, `CANCELLED`, `EXPIRED` (pending at the trade deadline)), `initiated_by_ai` (boolean DEFAULT FALSE), `ai_rationale` (text), `created_at` (timestamptz DEFAULT NOW()).
- **`napper_chat`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `league_id` (FK `napper_leagues(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `sender_type` (varchar(16) — `USER`, `AI_MANAGER`, `SYSTEM_BOT`), `sender_name` (varchar(64)), `sender_handle` (varchar(32)), `avatar_url` (text), `message_text` (text NOT NULL), `card_payload` (jsonb — Benchwarmer of the Week, High Scorer, Trade Alert, Waiver Claim cards), `created_at` (timestamptz DEFAULT NOW()).
- **`napper_drafts`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `league_id` (FK `napper_leagues(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE; persistent draft sessions belong to leagues), `season` (int, set to `league_state.current_season`), `rounds` (int DEFAULT 15), `teams_count` (int DEFAULT 10), `scoring_type` (varchar(16) DEFAULT 'PPR'), `status` (varchar(16) DEFAULT 'PENDING' — `PENDING`, `IN_PROGRESS`, `COMPLETED`), `current_round` (int DEFAULT 1), `current_pick` (int DEFAULT 1), `is_mock` (boolean DEFAULT FALSE), `user_slot` (int DEFAULT 1), `settings` (jsonb DEFAULT `{}`), `created_at` (timestamptz DEFAULT NOW()). Standalone mock drafts are client-side only and are not written to `napper_drafts` or `napper_draft_picks`; their picks are saved when the user creates a league.
- **`napper_draft_picks`**:
  - `id` (SERIAL PK), `draft_id` (FK `napper_drafts(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `round_num` (int NOT NULL), `pick_num` (int NOT NULL), `overall_pick` (int NOT NULL), `team_id` (FK `napper_teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE, nullable for mock drafts), `team_name` (varchar(64)), `slot_num` (int NOT NULL), `player_id` (FK `players(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE, nullable until selected), `picked_by` (varchar(16) DEFAULT 'AI' — `USER`, `AI`, `AUTO`), `pick_notes` (text), `created_at` (timestamptz DEFAULT NOW()). Unique constraint: `UNIQUE(draft_id, overall_pick)`.
- **Waivers** (`db/migrations/2026_12_05_napper_waivers.sql`, used by `engines/napper_waiver_engine.py`):
  - **`napper_waiver_claims`**: `id` (SERIAL PK), `league_id` (FK, CASCADE), `team_id` (FK `napper_teams`, CASCADE), `add_player_id` (FK `players`), `drop_player_id` (FK `players`, nullable), `rank` (smallint; the team's preference order among its pending claims), `status` (`PENDING`, `WON`, `LOST`, `INVALID`, `CANCELLED`), `reason` (`CLAIM` for the user's claims; AI pickups `NEED`, `STREAM`, `UPGRADE`), `note` (why a claim was lost or invalid), `week` (the waiver run that decided it), `created_at`, `processed_at`. The user's claims on players on waivers wait as `PENDING`; AI pickups are logged as `WON` when a run makes them.
  - **`napper_waiver_wire`**: players on waivers, i.e. dropped since the last run: `league_id`, `player_id` (PK together), `dropped_by_team_id`, `clears_week` (the run that decides them; unclaimed, they become free agents then), `dropped_at`.
  - **`napper_waiver_runs`**: one row per completed run, `(league_id, week)` PK, `priority` (int[]; team ids in claim order), `processed_at`. A re-run of the same week is skipped.


### 3.5 Media & Content Tables
- **`agent_memories`**:
  - `id`, `agent_id` (unique), `archetype`, `assigned_team_id`, `persistent_memory` (the agent's fixed identity line), `handle`, `display_name`, `updated_at`, `bio` (Agent Wire profile line), `followers` (simulated, set once by the social desk; NULL until it runs), `avatar_color` (unused until avatars are decided).
  - `db/migrations/2026_10_22_agent_wire_social.sql` gave every agent that posts a non-generic handle, display name and bio (approved by JR), renamed beat reporters whose templated surnames repeated (43) and same-team die-hards / bandwagon fans who shared a first name, and added the social accounts `The_Flex_Spot` (`FANTASY_GURU`, @TheFlexSpot), `Gridiron_Memes` (`NFL_MEMES`, @GridironMemes) and `Mock_Draft_Marty` (`DRAFT_SICKO`, @MockDraftMarty). `Skip_Bayless_National` (article byline) and `Skip_Bayless` (`SKIP_TWEET`) share @RealSkipBayless; Agent Wire shows them as one profile. `db/migrations/2026_10_25_former_players.sql` added the 20 `FORMER_PLAYER` accounts (agent ids `Former_<Name>`, §7.8).
- **`agent_memory_entries`** (§7.7): `id`, `agent_id`, `season`, `week`, `article_id` (FK `news_feed` ON DELETE CASCADE), `kind` ('TAKE'), `text`, `team_id`, `player_id`, `created_at`.
- **`agent_predictions`** (§7.7): `id`, `agent_id`, `season`, `week` (the predicted game's week), `article_id` (FK `news_feed` ON DELETE CASCADE), `game_id`, `label` (roundtable panellist or ''), `team_a_id`, `score_a`, `team_b_id`, `score_b`, `created_at`; unique `(article_id, game_id, label)`.
- **`agent_season_summaries`** (§7.7): `agent_id`, `season`, `through_week`, `summary`, `updated_at`; primary key `(agent_id, season, through_week)`.
- **`media_outputs`**:
  - `id`, `game_id` (FK to `games(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `content_type`, `payload_json` (jsonb), `created_at`.
- **`news_feed`**:
  - `id`, `season`, `week`, `team_id`, `content_type` ('ARTICLE' | 'TWEET'), `persona`, `article_type`, `slot`, `headline`, `excerpt`, `content`, `created_at`.
  - Agent Wire post columns (`db/migrations/2026_10_22_agent_wire_social.sql`; filled by the social desk, `docs/SOCIAL_MATRIX.md`): `agent_id` (author), `post_kind` (REACTION, PERFORMANCE, BAD_GAME, COMPARISON, TEAM_STORY, PLAYER, BEAT, STANDINGS, SHARE, REPLY, PREVIEW, MEME), `posted_at` (simulated in-world time; `created_at` stays the real generation time), `reply_to_id` (FK `news_feed` ON DELETE CASCADE), `linked_article_id` (FK `news_feed` ON DELETE SET NULL; the article a SHARE links), `subject_game_id`, `subject_player_id`, `likes`, `reposts`, `reply_count`, `views`, `hashtags` (text[]).
- **Record book** (`db/migrations/2026_10_26_nfl_records.sql`, `2026_10_28_league_record_book.sql`, `2026_11_23_postseason_records.sql`, §2.9): `record_categories` (`category` PK, `label`, `scope` SEASON / GAME / CAREER, `subject` PLAYER / TEAM / COACH, `better` HIGH / LOW, `sort_order`, `coverage` FULL / BOOK, `season_type` REGULAR / POSTSEASON); `franchise_records` (`team_id`, `category`, `value`, `holder` (player name, or "2007 New England Patriots"), `player_id`, `season`, `source` NFL / BOOK / LEAGUE; unique `(team_id, category, holder, season)`): every holder a franchise record has had (regular season only); `league_record_entries` (`category`, `value`, `holder`, `team_id` (the franchise under its current name), `player_id`, `season`, `game_date` (NFL single games), `game_id` (league single games), `source`): entries on the NFL's all-time top-10 lists (regular season and national postseason). Views: `franchise_records_current` (current holders, ties included), `league_top10` (`rank` computed, ties share it, exposing `season_type`) and `league_records` (per category, the best of the complete regular-season franchise records and the top of `league_top10`).
- **`social_trends`** (`docs/SOCIAL_MATRIX.md` §4): `id`, `season`, `week`, `rank`, `kind` (TEAM / PLAYER / GAME / HASHTAG), `key`, `title`, `category`, `description`, `volume` (simulated post count), `score`; unique `(season, week, kind, key)`. Empty until the social desk is built.
- **`article_teams`**:
  - `id` (PK, int), `article_id` (FK to `news_feed(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `team_id` (FK to `teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `mention_type` (varchar: 'PRIMARY' | 'FEATURED' | 'MENTIONED'), `mention_count` (int), `created_at` (timestamp), `UNIQUE(article_id, team_id)`.
- **`power_rankings`**:
  - `id` (PK), `season`, `week` (the week published for, i.e. entering that week), `rank` (1–32), `team_id` (FK to `teams(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `previous_rank` (rank in the latest earlier stored week of the season; NULL if none), `movement` (`previous_rank − rank`, positive = up; NULL if none), `record` (W-L snapshot from `team_seasons` at store time), `kicker`, `blurb`, `article_id` (FK to `news_feed(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `persona`, `created_at`. `UNIQUE(season, week, team_id)` and `UNIQUE(season, week, rank)`. Written only by `newsroom/power_rankings.store_power_rankings` (see 7.6).
- **`article_players`**:
  - `id` (PK, int), `article_id` (FK to `news_feed(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `player_id` (FK to `players(id)` ON DELETE CASCADE), `team_id` (FK to `teams(id)` ON DELETE SET NULL), `mention_type` (varchar: 'PRIMARY' | 'FEATURED' | 'MENTIONED'), `mention_count` (int), `created_at` (timestamp), `UNIQUE(article_id, player_id)`.

### 3.6 Triggers
- **`trigger_update_team_records`** (`AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON games`, function `update_team_records()`): on insert, or when `status`/scores change, recomputes both teams' `wins`/`losses` for that season from completed **regular-season** games and upserts them into `team_seasons` (playoff games don't count; `2026_11_04_playoff_games.sql`). Seasons before 2035 hold only their Super Bowl, so their stored records still show that one game until a row of theirs changes.
- **`trigger_sync_season_stats_team`** (`AFTER UPDATE OF team_id ON player_seasons`, function `sync_team_to_season_stats()`): when a player's team changes, moves their `season_stats` rows for that season (team NULL or the old team) to the new team, merging counters into an existing new-team row where one exists.

---

## 4. Backend Architecture (`server/api.py` - FastAPI)

The backend operates on Python 3.11+ using FastAPI, `uvicorn`, and `psycopg` (connection pooling with `dict_row`).

### 4.1 System & Schedules
- `GET /api/status`: Returns current league season and week from `league_state`, and `last_updated` (the latest `captures.loaded_at` of a loaded run; the Stats page's "Updated" stamp, hidden when null).
- `GET /api/schedule?season={season}&week={week}`:
  - Returns matchups for the week, records, kickoff times, home/away TWI (`home_twi`, `away_twi`), combined matchup `watchability`, and `excitement_index` on the 70–100 display scale. That is the final $E$ for completed games and the projection otherwise; `excitement_projected` is true for projections, and the value is null if a game hasn't been projected yet.
- `GET /api/standings/{season}`:
  - Division and conference standings, wins, losses, win percentage, streak, points for/against, and last 5 games.
- `GET /api/power_rankings?season={season}&week={week}`:
  - Stored weekly Power Rankings (7.6): `season`, `week`, `available_weeks`, the source `article` (`id`, `headline`, `excerpt`, `persona`, `created_at`), and `rankings` ordered 1–32 (`rank`, `previous_rank`, `movement`, `record`, `kicker`, `blurb`, `team_id`, `abbr`, `name`, `city`, `team_color`, `conference`, `division`). `season` defaults to the current season and `week` to its latest stored week; a week with no stored ranking returns an empty `rankings` list. The same article is also served by `GET /api/news/week/{week}` in `slotted.standalone`.

### 4.2 Team & Roster Hubs
- `GET /api/team/{abbr}?season={season}`: Team metadata (record, overall/offensive/defensive ratings, team color) and the season's `roster` (id, full name, jersey number, ESPN id, position, experience, OVR; best OVR first). `season` defaults to `league_state.current_season`.
- `GET /api/team/{abbr}/schedule?season={season}`: Full 18-week schedule with completed scores and upcoming dates.
- `GET /api/team/{abbr}/news?season={season}&limit={limit}`: Returns news articles and beat coverage specifically tagged for or assigned to a franchise (`article_teams` join or `news_feed.team_id`), prioritized by `mention_type` (`PRIMARY` > `FEATURED` > `MENTIONED`) and week recency, while strictly excluding opposing teams' beat reporters (`WHERE NOT (n.persona = 'LOCAL_BEAT_REPORTER' AND n.team_id IS DISTINCT FROM %s)`). When `limit` is omitted, all articles written for the franchise across the entire season are returned (powering the horizontally scrollable season feed). Author display names correctly reflect the author's franchise for beat writers. If the team's local beat hasn't been woken for the current week (no article filed under the team with a `Local Beat N` slot that week; national primers use `Slot 6–8` and don't count), it starts the on-demand local swarm wakeup (`SwarmDispatcher.dispatch_team_local`, `live=True`, `save_to_db=True`) in a background thread via `start_local_wakeup` (the check is `maybe_start_team_wakeup`, shared with the Agent Wire Team Hub) and returns the existing feed immediately; the new beat reports and the team's Agent Wire posts appear on a later load. Each (team, season, week) is attempted at most once every `LOCAL_WAKEUP_RETRY_SECONDS` (600s), which covers a run in progress and keeps repeat views from re-triggering a failed one. `NEWSROOM_AUTO_LOCAL_WAKEUP=0` disables it.
- `GET /api/team/{id}/season_stats/{season}`: Aggregated season totals (passing yards, rushing yards, points per game, points allowed per game).

### 4.3 Player Analytics, Global Search & Modal Overlays
- `GET /api/search?q={query}&season={season}&limit={limit}`:
  - Global search endpoint across all 32 NFL franchises and players for the main site search bar (`GlobalSearchModal`, keyboard shortcut ⌘K / Ctrl+K).
  - Teams match on name, city, abbreviation, or combined city + name; returns team logo, full name, division, and abbreviation.
  - Players match on full name; returns player ID, name, position, overall rating, jersey number, team abbreviation, team name, and ESPN ID.
- `GET /api/player/{id}?season={season}`:
  - Player personal information and ratings. Decoupled base player query gracefully falls back to historical `season_stats` or the player's most recent roster record when queried with a season prior to entry, preventing "Player not found" errors on past seasons.
  - Full 18-week `game_log` (accounting for bye weeks and completed game fantasy statistics). Switching `gameLogSeason` in `PlayerPage` immediately re-fetches the game log for that season.
  - Multi-season `career_stats` with games played, yards, touchdowns, and overall fantasy rank, plus each season's `season_stat_lines` defense (`def_*`), kicking (`kick_*`), punting (`punt_*`), returns (`kr_*`, `pr_*`) and `blk_sacks_allowed` with `has_capture_line`. Merges historical entries from `season_stats` (e.g. 2034, 2033, 2032) so past season totals appear in Career Stats.
  - Interactive season selection: on `PlayerPage`, selecting a season from the Game Log dropdown or clicking any row in the Career Stats table immediately switches the active season, updating the hero highlight card and game log.
  - `articles`: up to six newsroom articles that mention the player (`article_players`, the entity tagger's PRIMARY / FEATURED / MENTIONED tags), newest first, with id, season, week, headline, excerpt, short type label (the `article_types.json` name without a leading "The" or trailing parenthetical) and author display name. Opposing teams' beat reporters are strictly excluded so only national coverage and the player's own franchise beat writers appear. The player page's Player Coverage list shows them (linking to `#article-{id}`) and is hidden when there are none.
  - `stat_group`: `passing` (QB), `rushing` (HB/FB), `receiving` (WR/TE), `blocking` (OL), `kicking` (K), `punting` (P, LS) or `defense`. The player page (`PlayerPage`) uses it for its season card, game-log and career columns (defenders: tackles, sacks, TFL, INT, PD, FF, TD; kickers: FG, long, 50+, XP; punters: punts, average, net, inside the 20; linemen: sacks allowed), shows "—" for a game or season with no recorded line, and names every position (`POSITION_LABELS`).
  - `weekly_outlook` for the league's current week (`league_state.current_week`): opponent, home/away, kickoff, `projection`, `pos_rank`/`ecr_rank`, `ros_rank` and 1–5 `stars`, all from that week's `fantasy_projections` row (`projected: true`).
    - **Bye:** if the league plays that week but the player's team doesn't, it returns `bye: true`, `projection` "0.0", the stored `ros_rank`, and null ranks/stars (no opponent fields). The modal shows a "BYE WEEK" card.
    - **No stored row** (e.g. week 1): `projection` is a rough estimate (season PPR average × 0.85–1.15 by matchup stars), `projected: false`, and the ranks are null. The modal shows "—" rather than a rank.
    - If the season has no games in the current week (e.g. viewing a past season), it uses the team's last game of that season and that week's projection row.
- `GET /api/stats/player?season={season}&category={category}&season_type={regular|postseason}`:
  - `season_type` (default `regular`) filters games on `game_type` (`season_type_filter`): regular season only, or every non-`REGULAR_SEASON` game. For the regular season the defense, special-teams, Downs and Red Zone Defense tables come from Madden's Season Stats / Team Stats screens; for the postseason they sum the season's captured playoff box scores instead (`POSTSEASON_LINES` over `game_stat_lines` in `player_st_leaders(..., postseason=True)`; `team_situational_postseason` over real `team_stats` values). A playoff box score has conversions but no attempts and penalty yards but no count, so postseason Downs returns only 3D CONV, 4D CONV, 2PT, RZ ATT, RZ TD, RZ TD% and PEN YDS (null where a game didn't record one), and the page shows only those columns (`postseasonStatColumns`, filtered to what the API returned).
  - Passing, Rushing, Receiving and Scoring player leaderboards, summed from the player's `player_stats` game lines by `player_id` (a traded player's lines for every team), shown under the season's team.
  - Defense (Tackles, Sacks, Interceptions) and special teams (Kicking, Punting, Kick Returns, Punt Returns) from `season_stat_lines` (`PLAYER_ST_CATEGORIES` / `player_st_leaders` in `server/api.py`): Madden's listed leaders only, shown under the season's team.
- `GET /api/game/{id}`: the game with both teams' records, `stats` (each `player_stats` line), `team_stats` and, from `game_stat_lines`, `defense` (every defender with a line: tackles, sacks, TFL, INT, PD, FF, FR, TD; biggest playmakers first), `kicking` and `punting` (empty for games whose player stats weren't captured). `team_stats` rows go through `real_team_stats`: a field in `estimated_fields` is blanked unless it is in `exact_fields` (the writers' box-score rule), `box_score_captured` says whether the row came from a captured box score, and without one `first_downs` (a backfill 0) is blanked; `total_plays` is never captured and always blank. The estimator's arrays aren't returned.
- **Season and week defaults:** every endpoint whose `season` / `week` is omitted uses `league_state` (`season_week()` in `server/api.py`): the current season, and for it the current week; another season's default week is 18. Players on the game page (`GET /api/game/{id}`), the player page's game log and career, and the fantasy rankings, search and dashboard finishes are joined on `player_stats.player_id`, never by name.
- `GET /api/stats/team?season={season}&category={category}&season_type={regular|postseason}`:
  - Team leaderboards for the same four categories, summed from `team_stats` over completed games, plus `Downs` (third/fourth-down conversions and rates, two-point tries, red-zone trips/TDs/TD rate, penalties and yards) and `Red Zone Defense` (points allowed and per game, red-zone trips/TDs/FGs allowed, TD and scoring rate allowed) from the `team_seasons` Team Stats totals (`team_situational_table`).

### 4.4 Fantasy Analytics Endpoints
- `GET /api/fantasy/dashboard?season={season}&week={week}`:
  - **Articles:** `hero` and `articles` (the 3 front-page cards) come from the stored Mu Fantasy slate (`news_feed`, `article_type` `FANTASY_*`) for the week shown: the requested week, weeks after 18 show Week 18, and a week without a slate shows the latest earlier one (`articles_week`). Each card: `id`, `slot`, `label` (article type name), `title`, `excerpt`, `author` (agent display name, else `fantasy_writers.json`), `time` (~230 words/min), `updated`, `team` (most-tagged team, lower-case, or null). `more_analysis_count` = shelf articles behind "View all". With no stored slate, `articles_source` is `placeholder` and the old placeholder hero/cards are returned.
  - Top 3 marquee games (`excitement_games`). These are ranked by editorial interest: raw (projected) $E$, +3 for a division game, and +2 per team not yet mathematically eliminated (regular season). This is the same `rank_games_by_interest` used for matchup-primer picks. Each game has `score` (display scale), `projected`, and a `note` such as "Week 18 · Projected · Division game · Playoff race".
  - Waiver wire trends, weekly projections, preseason ADP movers, and rest-of-season rank snapshots. The ADP card reads only week 1 rows with a `player_id` (later weeks carry a copy of `adp_rank`).
  - **Social takes** (`social_takes`): the requested week's 3 most-liked Agent Wire posts by the fantasy accounts (The Flex Spot, Berry, Yates), no replies or shares (`social_api.fantasy_takes`): `{id, quote, author, handle, tag}`, where `tag` is the subject player's team and position ("MIN • WR") or the post type ("START / SIT"). Empty when the week has none.
- `GET /api/fantasy/articles?season={season}&week={week}`: every Mu Fantasy article for the week shown (same week rule as the dashboard): `front_page` (Hero, Fantasy 1–3) and `more_analysis` (shelf, in order), cards as above, plus `available_weeks` (weeks with a fantasy slate) and `week` (null when none). Article text comes from `GET /api/news/article/{id}`.
- `GET /api/fantasy/rankings/weekly?season={season}&week={week}&position={pos}&limit={limit}`:
  - `mode`: `final` when every game that week is completed (ranked by actual PPR points), otherwise `projected` from that week's `fantasy_projections` rows (players on bye are excluded), and `unavailable` if neither exists.
  - Dynamic positional ranking (`pos_rank` e.g. `QB1`, `RB1`).
  - Prior-week positional finish comparison (`trend`).
  - Exact 1–5 matchup stars.
  - FLEX view strictly excludes QBs and presents a dedicated `POS` column.
- `GET /api/fantasy/rankings/ros?season={season}&position={pos}&limit={limit}`:
  - Rest-of-season consensus rankings ordered by `ros_total_points DESC`.
  - Columns: `RANK`, `PLAYER`, `ROS RANK` (on FLEX), `SEASON SOS` (1–5 stars), `PLAYOFF SOS` (Weeks 15–17, 1–5 stars), `PROJECTED ROS FPTS`, and `TREND` (`adp_rank - overall_ros_rank`).
- `GET /api/fantasy/rankings/adp?season={season}&position={pos}&limit={limit}`:
  - Consensus preseason draft board ordered by `adp_rank ASC`.
  - Columns: `RANK`, `PLAYER`, `POSITION` (positional rank ordered by ADP, e.g. first RB is `RB1`, first WR is `WR1`), `TEAM (BYE)` (e.g. `CAR (12)`), `SEASON SOS`, and `PLAYOFF SOS`.
  - Position filter and week selectors omitted for an unobstructed full-draft view.
- `GET /api/fantasy/players?season={season}&min_points={min_points}&q={query}`:
  - Returns fantasy skill players (`QB`, `HB`, `FB`, `WR`, `TE`, `K`) for the season, left-joining completed games and projections so that in preseason or unplayed weeks, players are returned with projected fantasy points rather than being dropped.
  - Powers zero-latency client search in `FantasyHeader`, supporting keyboard shortcuts (⌘K), outside click dismissal, and Enter key selection.
- **Agent Wire** (`server/social_api.py`, §7.8). Only posts written by the social desk (with an `agent_id`) are served. `season` defaults to `league_state.current_season`; `week` to the latest week with posts. A post is `{id, season, week, kind, kind_label, text, posted_at, likes, reposts, replies (simulated + real), real_replies, views, hashtags, team_abbr, local, subject_game_id, subject_player_id, author {agent_id, handle, display_name, archetype, label, team_abbr, team_color, followers}, reply_to {id, handle, display_name} | null, article {id, headline, excerpt, article_type} | null}`.
  - `GET /api/social/feed?season&week&tab=foryou|latest&team={ABBR}&trend={KIND:KEY}&limit=30&offset=0`: the week's top-level posts (no replies). For You = likes + 2 × reposts + 3 × replies, times a recency weight from 1.0 (the week's newest post) to 0.5; Latest = `posted_at` descending. Returns `weeks` (weeks with posts), `as_of` (the newest post's time, the feed's "now" for relative times), `total` and `next_offset`.
  - `GET /api/social/post/{id}`: `{post, parent, replies}` (replies oldest first).
  - `GET /api/social/profile/{handle}?season&limit&offset`: `{account {handle, display_name, bio, label, archetype, team_abbr, team_color, followers, following, agent_ids}, post_count, posts}`; accounts that share a handle (Skip's byline and tweet account) are one profile.
  - `GET /api/social/trends?season&week&limit=20`: the week's `social_trends` rows, each with `id` = `KIND:KEY` for the feed's `trend` filter.
  - `GET /api/social/search?q&season&limit=40`: posts whose text, author or tagged team matches, newest first, plus up to 8 matching accounts (one per handle).
  - `GET /api/social/suggestions?season&week&limit=3`: Who to follow, the accounts whose posts drew the most likes that week.
  - `GET /api/social/team/{abbr}?season&week&tab=latest`: a Team Hub (the feed filtered to the team, plus `team {id, abbr, name, city, team_color, division, record}`). Viewing it starts the team's local wakeup through `maybe_start_team_wakeup`, the same check as the team news page; `wakeup_started` says whether it did.

### 4.4b Napper Interactive Fantasy Endpoints (`server/napper_api.py`, `/api/napper/*`)
High-performance RESTful API powering the Napper interactive multi-league Sleeper-style application:
- **Post-game keys on player payloads** (team and matchup): once the player's NFL game that week is `COMPLETED`, `game_result` ("Final W 37-34 @ LAR": result, the player's team score first, `@` away / `vs` home, opponent) and `stat_line`, a Sleeper-style box score from `format_stat_line` (`_weekly_game_lines`, `server/napper_api.py`): QB/RB passing ("12/19 CMP, 210 YD, 1 TD, 1 INT"), rushing ("10 CAR, 121 YD, 1 TD") then receiving ("5 REC, 56 YD"; no targets are tracked), WR/TE receiving first, plus "N FUM"; K "3/4 FG, LNG 53, 2/2 XP"; DEF from `game_stat_defense` "2 SACK, 1 INT, 1 FR, 1 TD, 1 SFTY, 1 BLK, 17 PA" (zero categories left out; "No stats" when a skill player had no line). Both are null before kickoff and on byes.
- **Injury keys on player payloads** (team, matchup, `teams_rosters`, waivers, trades): `injury_status` (`IR` = on injured reserve, `O` = out, `null` = healthy), `injury_type` (e.g. `UPPER ARM FRACTURE`), `injury_duration` (the report's text, e.g. `2 - 4 WEEKS`), `injury_weeks_remaining`. They come from `engines/napper_injuries.get_injury_map` for the payload's season and week: the player's latest `injuries` report counted down, the same rule that zeroes his projection, so a player shown as out is also projected at 0.
- `GET /api/napper/profile`: Returns authenticated user profile (`id`, `handle` `@jrereyi2000`, `display_name` "The Straw Hat Pirates", `avatar_url`, `trophies`) and badge count for incoming `pending_trades_count`.
- `GET /api/napper/leagues`: Returns only non-seed leagues containing the active user's team, with the active simulation season/week and live roster-derived current matchup scores, projections, opponent name, and unplayed starter count.
- `POST /api/napper/leagues/create`: Creates a new custom league (`{name, teams_count, scoring_type, is_superflex, status, archetype_preset, archetype_composition}`) for the active simulation season/week. Adds the user team and AI managers across 12 canonical archetypes, and creates a head-to-head schedule. New in-season leagues start with empty rosters and zero records/scores; players and actual fantasy points are never invented to fill them. Seed leagues remain stored but are filtered from the active league list.
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/set_strategies`: Configures and re-assigns manager strategies and AI team personas only while a league is `PRE_DRAFT` (`{archetype_composition: dict, archetype_preset: str, archetypes: list}`), broadcasting an automated `STRATEGY_UPDATE` card announcement in `napper_chat`. Returns HTTP 409 after the draft stage.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}`: Returns league metadata, roster format specifications, and standings calculated from weekly starter points through the active week. PF/PA sum each team's and its scheduled opponents' starter fantasy points; W-L-T includes completed regular-season matchups; seeds rank record percentage first, then PF.
- `DELETE /api/napper/league/{id}`: Deletes the selected league and its dependent teams, rosters, matchups, drafts, trades, and chat. The league switcher confirms the action; deleting the last active league leaves the create/join empty state.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/teams_rosters?week={week}`: Returns all teams in the league with their active starting and bench rosters, projections, and headshots for the trade market and drafting.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/team?week={week}`: Returns the active user's team and its saved league/week lineup, including player IDs, ESPN IDs, active-season weekly projections, actual game opponent/kickoff/status, and actual weekly fantasy points. Slots come from the league's `roster_format` (starters, then `BN`, then `IR` slots, shown in the Team tab's Injured Reserve section); unconfigured Superflex slots are omitted. Lineup changes persist through the lineup endpoint.
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/team/name`: Renames the signed-in user's team in the selected league (`{team_name}`; 1–64 trimmed characters), and returns the updated team name.
- **Playoffs** (`engines/napper_playoffs.py`, the same bracket as `fantasy_league_sim`): once week 14 is final, the top 6 by record then points for (`_get_league_standings`) are seeded and `napper_teams.seed` is written. Week 15 quarterfinals 3 v 6 and 4 v 5 (seeds 1–2 bye); week 16 semifinals seed 1 v the lowest seed left, seed 2 v the other winner; week 17 final (higher seed listed home). Ties go to the higher seed (`fantasy_league_sim.playoff_round`). `sync_playoffs(cur, league_id)` writes each round's `napper_matchups` (`playoff_round`) once the week before it is final, replaces any other rows in that week, and once the final is final sets `napper_leagues.champion_team_id` and adds a trophy to the champion's `napper_user_profiles.trophies` (human team only); "Napper Commissioner" posts `PLAYOFF_BRACKET` (seeds set) and `CHAMPION` cards. Idempotent; called by the weekly step, by `_ensure_matchup_and_rosters` for weeks 15–17 (which no longer makes placeholder pairings there), and by the endpoints below. Leagues with fewer than 6 teams have no playoffs. `team_week_note` explains a missing playoff matchup (bracket not set yet, bye, missed the playoffs, eliminated).
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/playoffs`: The bracket: `status` (`PROJECTED` before week 14 is final: seeds are the standings so far; `IN_PROGRESS`; `COMPLETE`; `NONE` under 6 teams), `playoff_teams`, `byes` (seeds 1–2), `rounds` (weeks 15–17: `round`, `label`, `games` of `{home, away, winner_team_id}`, each side `{team_id, seed, team_name, is_user, score (null before the week's games start), proj}`; projected quarterfinals while `PROJECTED`), `champion`, `finish` (`{team_id: MISSED | QUARTERFINAL | SEMIFINAL | RUNNER_UP | CHAMPION}` as decided).
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/scores?week={week}`: Returns every scheduled fantasy matchup for the selected week with each team's calculated season record and seed, plus `playoff_round` per matchup and a `note` when a playoff week has no matchups yet. Team scores are calculated from the actual weekly points of starting players (PPR, half-PPR, or standard reception scoring, plus kicker and team-defense scoring); projections sum the stored weekly player projections. Stored placeholder scores are ignored.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/matchup?week={week}&matchup_id={matchup_id}`: Returns the user's matchup by default or the specified league matchup for the selected week, with saved roster slots, actual NFL opponent/kickoff/status, active-season projections, live weekly player points, season records, and each team's share of combined points (projections before games begin, actual points after scoring starts). Standard 1-QB leagues omit `SUPERFLEX`; configured Superflex leagues include it. If a starter slot is vacated, eligible bench players are automatically slotted into available starting spots. Historical weeks are not populated by copying a different week's roster; only the active week can inherit the preceding lineup when no current lineup exists. In weeks 15–17 a user without a playoff game gets a 404 whose `detail` says why (`team_week_note`) instead of another team's matchup.
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/lineup`: Atomically validates and applies lineup swaps between starting and bench slots (`{team_id, week, swaps: [{player_id, new_slot}]}`). Enforces position eligibility and roster slot limits (including `roster_format.IR`), and the active roster limit: moving a player off IR onto a full roster (starters + `BN`) is a 400 until a player is dropped. A player can move into `IR` only when IR-eligible (decision D1, `engines/napper_injuries.is_ir_eligible`): on injured reserve, or out at least `IR_MIN_WEEKS` = 2 more weeks by the latest injury report (counted down from the report week, as in the projection engine); otherwise 400. Then refreshes every matchup of that week (`refresh_matchup_projections`: projections = the starters' `projected_points`, excluding BN/IR; `win_prob_home` = home share).
- **Has a game** (League Transactions §3.3e): everywhere Napper decides whether a player plays that week (AI lineups, the waiver pool, the roster/matchup/players views' opponent and game time, post-game card lines) the player's `player_seasons.team_id` must play and `released_week` must be NULL or after that week. A released player keeps his last team and stays visible (rostered free agents aren't hidden), shown as having no game.
- **AI lineups** (`engines/napper_lineup_engine.py`, not an endpoint; run weekly): for each AI team and week, (1) IR-eligible players fill the IR slots by `ros_total_points` and players no longer eligible return to the bench; (2) if the active roster exceeds starting slots + `BN`, the lowest-ROS bench players are dropped, never one the lineup needs (decision D3); (3) QB, RB, WR, TE, K, DEF, FLEX, SUPERFLEX are filled greedily by `projected_points` among players who can play (not out per `napper_injuries`, not on bye), falling back to the best unavailable player rather than an empty slot; everyone else is benched. The lineup is written to that week and every later saved week, matchup projections are refreshed, and IR moves/drops are posted to `napper_chat` by "Napper Lineup Bot"; dropped players go on waivers. The lineup engine signs no free agents: unfillable slots, bye streaming and upgrades are the waiver run's job (below). Human teams are never changed (D5). Re-running a week is a no-op.
- **Free agency and waivers** (`engines/napper_waiver_engine.py`): a player dropped by any team (the user, an AI roster-limit drop, a waiver-run swap) goes on waivers (`napper_waiver_wire`) until the next weekly waiver run and can only be claimed; every other unrostered player is a free agent the user can add at once. AI managers act only in the run. **Roster limit:** the active roster (every player not in `IR`) is at most the starting slots + `BN` (15 in the default format); `IR` doesn't count.
- **Waiver run** (`run_waivers(cur, league_id, week)`, once per league and week, in the weekly step from week 2, after the first AI lineup pass): priority = reverse standings (`_get_league_standings` reversed: worst record, then fewest points for, first), the same order every round. In each round each team gets one add: the user's team its next pending claim that can go through (taken player → `LOST` "claimed by another team"; drop no longer rostered or roster over the limit → `INVALID`); an AI team its next pickup, decided on its turn from what's left (`ai_next_claim`): **NEED** a starting slot no rostered player can fill (e.g. the only QB is on IR) gets the best healthy free agent at that position by value (always); **STREAM** a starting slot whose only candidates can't play this week (bye or out) gets the free agent with the best `projected_points` who plays, if the dropped player's value is lower (only in weeks the manager streams; not capped); **UPGRADE** an open spot gets the best healthy free agent by value, or the lowest-value spare is swapped for one worth more than `value × 1.15 + 1` (positions under `upgrade_cap`: QB = QB slots + 2 × SUPERFLEX slots, RB/WR 7, TE 2, K/DEF 1), up to the week's upgrade budget. **Managers** (ported from `fantasy_league_sim`): each AI team has a fixed `activity` (0.2–1.0) and `reactivity` (0–0.5), drawn from its team id (`manager_traits`; not stored). Personas lean: `WAIVER_HAWK` activity 0.7–1.0, `DIE_HARD` 0.2–0.5; `ANALYTICS_NERD` reactivity 0–0.15, `TRASH_TALKER` 0.3–0.5, `UNDERDOG` 0.25–0.5. Each week (`week_plan`, seeded per league, week and team) the upgrade budget = how many of 2 draws (3 for `WAIVER_HAWK`) fall under `activity`, and the manager streams with probability min(1, `activity` + 0.5). **Value** = `ros_total_points` scaled to the fantasy weeks left (`fantasy_league_sim.ros_base`: `ros × (17 − week + 1) / (18 − week + 1)`; from week 15, `projected_points × (17 − week + 1)`) × (1 + `reactivity` × `hot_signal`: last week's actual PPR vs. `avg_points`, `(last − avg) / (avg + 5)` clamped −0.5..1, 0 if he didn't play). A full roster drops its lowest-value spare (outside this week's best lineup, not added in this run; decision D3). Rounds repeat until nobody adds. Adds land on the bench for this and every later saved week; players dropped in the run stay on waivers until the next run; unclaimed players whose waivers clear become free agents. Each add is posted as a `WAIVER_CLAIM` card by "Waiver Bot" (`reason` in the payload).
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/waivers?position={pos}&q={query}`: Returns available active-season players for the current week (top 60 by projection; `position=RB` matches the roster's `HB`/`FB`/`RB`), with database player IDs, ESPN IDs, stored weekly projections, season averages, opponent/kickoff details, matchup stars, rest-of-season ranks, and `on_waivers` / `waiver_week` (the run that decides a player on waivers). Missing projections and game details remain unavailable rather than using fabricated placeholders. Also returns the user's side of the Players tab: `team_id`, `roster` (`{active, limit, ir, ir_limit}`), `waiver_priority` (`{position, teams, next_run_week}`), `claims` (as below) and `my_players` (the user's roster, lowest ROS first, for the drop picker).
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/waivers/claim`: Adds a player (`{team_id, week, add_player_id, drop_player_id}`; `team_id` 0 = the user's team). A free agent is added at once to the bench for this and later weeks (the drop goes on waivers until the next run), posted as a "Free agency" `WAIVER_CLAIM` card, and the week's matchup projections are refreshed; returns `status: "success"`. A player on waivers becomes a `PENDING` claim for the next run (next `rank`; one pending claim per player) and returns `status: "queued"` with `claim_id` and `waiver_week`. 400 when the player is already rostered, the drop isn't on the roster, or the add would put the active roster over the limit without a drop.
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/waivers/drop`: Drops a player from the user's team (`{team_id, player_id, week}`) for this and later weeks; he goes on waivers until the next run. Posts a chat card and refreshes matchup projections.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/waivers/claims`: The user's `pending` claims (rank order) and `last_run`: every claim decided in the league's latest waiver run (all teams, with `status`, `reason`, `note`).
- `DELETE /api/napper/league/{id}/waivers/claims/{claim_id}`: Cancels one of the user's pending claims (`CANCELLED`); 404 if it isn't pending.
- **Trade deadline** (`engines/napper_trade_engine.py`): trades are open through week `TRADE_DEADLINE_WEEK` = 11 (`trades_open(week)`, judged by `league_state.current_week`). From week 12 the engine proposes and processes nothing, the propose and accept endpoints return 400, and `expire_pending_trades` marks the league's pending offers `EXPIRED` and posts one `TRADE_DEADLINE` notice by "Napper Trade Bot" (run by the weekly step, and lazily by the trades endpoints). Waivers and free agency stay open.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/trades`: Returns all trade proposals for the league with player cards, status badges, and AI reasoning rationales, plus `trade_deadline_week`, `trades_open` and `current_week`.
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/trades/propose`: Creates a pending trade offer (`{proposer_team_id, target_team_id, offered_player_ids, requested_player_ids, ai_rationale}`); 400 after the trade deadline. Players in an `IR` slot can't be traded (400, decision D2); accepting a trade re-checks this, and the AI trade engine skips IR players when building and accepting offers.
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/trades/{trade_id}/respond`: Handles trade acceptance or rejection (`{response: "ACCEPT" | "REJECT"}`). After the trade deadline a pending offer can't be answered: it expires and the call returns 400. Acceptance atomically mutates rosters across current and future weeks (traded players arrive on the bench), then `fill_empty_starter_slots` (`engines/napper_lineup_engine.py`) moves each **AI** team's best eligible bench player (that week's `projected_points`, then `ros_total_points`) into any starting slot the trade emptied, for every saved week from the trade week on; the human team's lineup is never changed. It logs a `TRADE_ALERT` announcement in `napper_chat`. AI trade evaluation (`process_ai_to_ai_trades`, pending offers to any AI team; the same refill runs after acceptance) values players with the league season's positions and projections, and calculates net remaining positional starters (outgoing minus incoming talent) so fair positional swaps do not trigger orphan starter rejections.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/chat?limit={limit}`: Returns league chat messages and bot cards in ascending chronological order (querying the most recent messages up to `limit`).
- `POST /api/napper/league/{id}/chat/send`: Posts user chat message or custom payload to the league chat stream.
- `POST /api/napper/drafts/create_mock`: Retired with HTTP 410; standalone mock drafts are initialized and managed in the client and do not create database rows. The mock draft player pool is read from active-season player data. Saving a mock draft as a league persists the new league, draft, and picks.
- `GET /api/napper/draft/{id}`: Returns real-time draft room state, board picks matrix, active on-the-clock team, teams list, and remaining timer.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/claim_slot`: Updates user's draft slot (`{slot}`) in a pending draft, atomically swapping team assignments and updating pick ownership names on the board while keeping archetype assignments intact.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/randomize_order`: Randomizes draft slot assignments for all teams (`{keep_user_slot: bool = true}`). When `keep_user_slot` is false, randomizes the user's slot as well; re-assigns draft board pick team ownership across all rounds while preserving draft progress.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/set_order`: Sets custom draft slot order for all teams (`{slot_order?: List[int], teams?: List[dict]}`). Atomically re-indexes slots 1..N, relocates user draft slot, and re-assigns snake draft pick ownership across all rounds while preserving draft state.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/set_archetypes`: Configures the distribution of AI manager archetypes across bot slots (`{archetype_composition: dict, archetypes: list}`). Re-assigns AI manager personas and banter styles in-flight.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/start`: Transitions draft status from `PENDING` to `IN_PROGRESS`.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/pick`: Submits a draft pick (`{player_id}`), advances draft clock, and posts commentary in `napper_chat`.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/sim_step`: Simulates a single AI bot draft turn aligned directly with `engines/fantasy_league_sim.py` economic draft principles (`engines/napper_draft_engine.py`):
  - **Strict Roster Caps (`DRAFT_CAP`)**: Enforces max 2 QBs, max 2 TEs, max 7 RBs, max 7 WRs in 1-QB leagues (3 QBs permitted only if `is_superflex: true`).
  - **VORP Positional Weighting (`POS_WEIGHT`)**: Discounts raw projections by positional supply (`QB: 0.4`, `TE: 0.8`, `RB: 1.0`, `WR: 1.0`, `K/DEF: 0.2`), preventing raw QB points from swamping starting WRs and TEs.
  - **Backup Penalties (`BACKUP_PENALTY`)**: Applies heavy diminishing-return deductions (`-350 * qb_cnt`, `-250 * te_cnt`, `-60 * (depth - 3)`) once a starter is secured.
  - **Forced Lineup Filling (`forced = total_need >= picks_left`)**: Completely locks out filled positions when remaining draft picks are needed to satisfy empty starting slots (`QB: 1, RB: 2, WR: 2, TE: 1, K: 1, DEF: 1`).
  - **Canonical Strategy Modeling**: Distinct evaluation logic for all 6 core draft strategies (`BPA`, `HERO_RB`, `ZERO_RB`, `RB_HEAVY`, `EARLY_QB`, `EARLY_TE`) alongside manager personas.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/sim_to_user`: Simulates all AI picks until user is back on the clock or draft completes.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/sim_all`: Fast-forwards all remaining picks to completion.
- `POST /api/napper/draft/{id}/finalize`: Finalizes draft, generates letter-grade report cards, identifies best value and biggest reach, assigns starting and bench slots in `napper_rosters`, and generates round-robin schedule.
- `POST /api/napper/drafts/save_as_league`: Converts and persists a completed live or mock draft into an active, functional fantasy league in PostgreSQL (`napper_leagues` status `IN_SEASON`, `is_seed: false`, current week 1, 14-week schedule, optimal starters & bench slots, chat announcement).
- `GET /api/napper/draft/{id}/available_players?search={q}&position={pos}&limit={limit}&offset={offset}`: Returns only players with an active current-season `player_seasons` row, eligible fantasy position, and active NFL team. Season comes from `league_state.current_season`; player projections, age, and ADP come from that same season, while bye week is derived from its NFL schedule. Missing projection, age, or ADP values remain unavailable. The PTS column uses rest-of-season projected points and AVG uses the current week's fantasy projection. There is no fabricated rostered percentage. When `id=0`, returns the active-season pool without filtering previously drafted picks; live draft IDs exclude their selected players and stale-season drafts are rejected.

- `GET /api/fantasy/projections?season={season}&week={week}&position={pos}&limit={limit}`: Returns stored fantasy projections and consensus ADP rankings for fantasy-eligible positions (`QB`, `RB`, `WR`, `TE`, `K`, `DEF`), with `projections.pts` representing season total fantasy points and `projections.avg` representing weekly points per game (PPG). Defaults limit to 600.
- `GET /api/napper/league/{id}/draft`: Retrieves existing draft session or initializes a new draft session for a league in `PRE_DRAFT` status.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_napper_api.py`, `tests/test_napper_draft.py`, `tests/test_napper_draft_alignment.py`, `tests/test_napper_engines.py`, `tests/test_napper_e2e.py`.

### 4.5 Debug Endpoints (`server/debug_api.py`, the `#debug` page)
Reads are open like the rest of the API; every write needs header `X-Debug-Token` = `DEBUG_TOKEN` (env or `.env`; 403 when unset, 401 when wrong). The module opens its own connections (`DATABASE_URL`).
- `GET /api/debug/state`: `league_state`, the database name, the last 10 `captures` rows, `writes_enabled`, `running_job`, and `resumable`: up to 15 run folders with a `weekly_run.log` that aren't loaded (`folder`, `mode` weekly | games, `stage` capture incomplete | captured | extracted), newest first. `PUT /api/debug/state` `{current_season, current_week}` (week 1–23).
- `GET /api/debug/news`: summary of articles and Agent Wire posts grouped by season and week (`summary`: `[{season, week, articles, tweets}]`), plus `total_articles`, `total_tweets`, `current_season`, and `current_week`.
- `POST /api/debug/news/clear` `{season?: int, week?: int, content_type: "ARTICLE" | "ALL" | "TWEET", backup?: bool}`: clears matching `news_feed` content for rerun (cascading to `article_teams`, `article_players`, `power_rankings`, `agent_memory_entries`, and `agent_predictions`; clears `agent_season_summaries` through that week and `social_trends` when posts are deleted). Automatically takes a `pg_dump` data backup to `db/backups/` before deleting. Returns `{deleted, scope, remaining_articles, remaining_tweets, backup_file}`.
- `POST /api/debug/classify_article` `{prompt: str, season?: int, week?: int}`: classifies a story prompt via Gemini Flash (`gemini-3.8-flash`) into `target_desk` (`NATIONAL` or `FANTASY`), `article_type_id`, `writer_archetype`, `team_id`, and `desk_note`, returning the proposal along with full catalogs of available types, writers, and NFL teams for client dropdown overrides.
- `POST /api/debug/commission_article` `{prompt, season, week, target_desk, article_type_id, writer_archetype, team_id?, desk_note?, slot?}`: starts a background job running `newsroom.custom_commission` with real-time log streaming in `captures/jobs/`. Once complete, the job object surfaces `url_hash` (`#article-{id}` for national, `#article-fantasy-{id}` for fantasy), headline, and article ID.
- `GET /api/debug/transactions?season&week&limit (≤1000, default 200)`: League Transactions (§3.3e) for the season (default the current one; one week with `week`), newest first, with team abbreviations; plus `unparsed` and `ambiguous` rows (latest 100 each). `installed: false` when the migration isn't applied.
- `GET /api/debug/tables`: every table and view in `public` (`name`, `kind`, estimated `rows`, `columns`, `pk`).
- `GET /api/debug/tables/{name}?page&page_size (≤200)&sort&dir&q&filters`: rows plus `columns` (`column`, `type`, `nullable`, `generated`, `pk`), `pk`, `total`. `q` searches every column as text; `filters` is a JSON object `{column: value}`: plain text = case-insensitive contains, `=`, `!=`, `>`, `<`, `>=`, `<=` compare (value cast to the column's type), `null` / `!null`. Default order is the primary key.
- `PATCH /api/debug/tables/{name}` `{key: {pk columns}, changes: {column: value | null}}`: base tables with a primary key only; generated and key columns refused; values cast to the column type; table triggers fire as usual (e.g. a `games` score edit updates records). Returns the updated row.
- `GET /api/debug/endpoints`: every route (`path`, `methods`, `name`, docstring line, path and query params). `GET /api/debug/docs/{spec|migration|frontend}`: that Markdown file.
- Jobs (records, logs and exit codes in `captures/jobs/<id>.json|.log|.exit`): `POST /api/debug/jobs` `{kind: scan_week | backfill_week | game_scan | season_scan | roster_scan | contract_scan | transactions_scan | draft_scan | publish_articles | custom_commission, resume?: <captures folder>, week?: int}` starts `pipeline.weekly_capture` (`--no-articles` / `--mode games` / `--mode season` / `--mode roster` / `--mode contracts` / `--mode prospects`, `--resume`; Scan week becomes a playoff run on its own from week 19 and an articles-only run at week 22 (its note on the page says so), and the resume picker labels such folders `playoff`) or `newsroom.weekly_run` (`publish_articles`, accepting optional `week`) in its own process session (survives an API reload; `sh` writes the exit code); 409 while another job runs. `GET /api/debug/jobs` (last 20, status `running` / `done` / `failed` / `stopped` / `lost`, log tail, `url_hash` / `headline` on completed article commissions), `GET /api/debug/jobs/{id}/log?lines=`, `POST /api/debug/jobs/{id}/stop` (SIGINT to the job's process group: madden_capture releases every key and stops).
- Control page (`#debug` Control tab): provides a **League transactions** card (unparsed and ambiguous rows, latest rows; `GET /api/debug/transactions`) and the **League transactions scan** run, league state controls, a dedicated **Newsroom & Articles** rerun card (target week picker, scope toggle between articles only and articles + posts, clear button with confirmation, and one-click newsroom publish for instant rerun), an interactive Two-Step **Commission Custom Story** card (Step 1: prompt textarea + season/week input with Gemini analysis; Step 2: review/tweak card with desk toggle, article type, writer, team, and mandatory desk note directives, plus direct links to completed articles), on-demand runs with resume picker, capture history, and real-time streaming job logs.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_debug_api.py`, `tests/test_custom_commission.py` (token, start, log, one at a time, failure, stop, resume name, publish articles job with week, news summary, article clearance with backup, prompt classification, and dry-run national/fantasy commissioning).

---

## 5. Frontend & UI Architecture (`Mu_Network`)

Built on **React 19**, **TypeScript 5.7**, and **Vite 8**, featuring responsive layouts styled in `src/index.css`.

### 5.1 Shared Navigation & Hash Routing
Navigation uses a lightweight hash router (`window.location.hash`):
- `#`: Gridiron Newsroom Home (featured stories, analysis, trending topics, commemorative historic record spotlight, interactive edition-bar week picker to browse and read any week's articles).
- `#article-{id}`: Full reading view for stored newsroom articles (`/api/news/article/{id}`) and local/national beats, rendered via in-app markdown (`ArticleMarkdown`) with structured typography for headings (`<h2>`–`<h4>`), bullet and numbered lists (`<ul>`/`<ol>`), callout blockquotes with decorative quotation glyphs, responsive tables, horizontal dividers, inline formatting (`**bold**`, `*italic*`, `` `code` ``, `[link](url)`), and preserved drop-cap styling on the lead paragraph. The "Keep Reading" rail dynamically displays real related articles published that same week (`related_articles`). Author bylines and author bio notes link directly to the writer's `#author-{handle}` profile page.
- `#author-{handle}`: Dedicated full-page Author Profile page showing writer initials avatar, display name, handle (`@...`), role/archetype badge, assigned franchise badge (for beat reporters), bio, total articles count, and a comprehensive archive grid of all articles authored across weeks and seasons with links to `#article-{id}`.
- `#team-{abbr}`: Team hub with dynamic team colors, division rank, upcoming schedule, roster, and team stats. The "Team Coverage" section features the franchise spotlight hero card and a horizontally scrollable carousel displaying all beat and national articles written for that team across the season (opposing teams' beat reporters strictly excluded).
- `#schedule`: Full interactive 18-week league schedule.
- `#game-{id}`: Game view (`GamePage`). Team Stats: the score line (quarter by quarter when the box score was captured), yards, touchdowns and turnovers, and a Situational block (first downs, third/fourth-down and two-point conversions, red zone TDs/FGs and trips, penalties, time of possession) showing only rows with a real value for at least one team. Player Stats: passing, rushing and receiving cards, plus Defense, Kicking and Punting cards when the game's player stats were captured.
- `#stats-team` & `#stats-player`: League statistical leaderboards. The Season dropdown (both modes) sends `season_type`; the table header names the choice. Tabs are grouped by unit (`statCategoryGroups`): players get Offense, Defense and Special Teams; teams get Offense (with Downs) and Defense (Red Zone Defense). Unit columns are in `unitStatColumns` (`tests/test_api_defense_stats.py` checks they match the API), and defense/special-teams tables are labelled as the league's listed leaders.
- `#standings` & `#rankings`: Division standings and power rankings. `PowerRankingsPage` reads `GET /api/power_rankings`: one button per stored week, each team's rank, logo, record, last-week rank and movement ("NEW" when there is no previous week), kicker and blurb, with an empty state when nothing is stored.
- `#fantasy`: Mu Fantasy Analytics Command Center (accessible via top-nav "Fantasy" dropdown → "Analytics").
- `#napper` & `#napper-{leagueId}`: Napper Interactive Fantasy Platform (accessible via top-nav "Fantasy" dropdown → "Napper"). Dedicated Sleeper-style multi-league experience with 3-panel layout: Left Rail (single "Back to Home" button above Trade Proposals, league switcher & user profile), Center Stage (5 tabs: `MATCHUP`, `TEAM`, `PLAYERS`, `LEAGUE`, `TRADES`), and Right Rail (living league chat stream with autonomous manager banter and automated bot award cards). The weekly scoreboard is part of League.
- `#napper-draft` & `#napper-draft-{leagueId}`: Napper Interactive Draft Room and Mock Draft Simulator (accessible via left rail "Mock Draft Room", pre-draft league banners, and direct URL). Standalone mock drafts are client-side only and write no draft rows; creating a league from one persists the league, draft, and picks. League draft sessions remain database-backed. Clicking player names, headshots, draft-board picks, roster entries, queue entries, or recap player links opens the same Napper-themed Analytics-structured `NapperPlayerModal` used throughout Napper. Implements a snaking draft board matrix (`DraftBoardGrid.tsx`) supporting configurable league composition (8, 10, or 12 teams, customizable 12–16 rounds, user draft slot selector and column header `CLAIM` pills, PPR scoring by default, with Superflex enabled only when the league roster format includes that slot). Draft slots are randomized by default on initialization and support dynamic reshuffling via `🎲 Randomize Order` while still letting the user claim any specific slot without generating duplicate draft orders. Features a dedicated Draft Order modal (`DraftOrderModal.tsx`, accessed via `↕ Set Order` in the config bar or board header) that lets users manually set the draft order for all teams before the draft begins via drag-and-drop handles, direct slot selector dropdowns, step arrows, order reversal, or randomized order shuffle, prominently displaying each AI agent's strategy archetype with color-coded badges, tooltips, and in-modal archetype selectors while preserving team personas during live draft queries. Features an interactive Archetype Composition modal (`ArchetypeCompositionModal.tsx`) with 1-click presets ("Balanced Mix", "Trader's Paradise", "Sleeper Upside", "Analytics Heavy", "Zero RB Lab", "Hero RB Gauntlet", "Chaos / Trash Talk") and custom manager steppers configuring all 8 AI bot personas (`ANALYTICS_NERD`, `ZERO_RB_ZEALOT`, `HERO_RB`, `TRASH_TALKER`, `WAIVER_HAWK`, `DIE_HARD`, `TRADE_FIEND`, `UNDERDOG`), complete with colored badges across board headers and roster views. Features exact mathematical snake turns (slot $N$ back-to-back at rounds $1 \to 2$, slot 1 back-to-back at rounds $2 \to 3$) and directional serpentine arrows (`→`/`↓` on odd rounds, `←`/`↓` on even rounds). Floating up and down arrow controls toggle the bottom draft selection box open or closed, smoothly expanding the snaking draft board to full height (`.draft-board-wrapper.expanded`) with an interactive status pill to maximize board visibility during draft rounds. Integrates a filterable player pool queried live from `league_state.current_season` (up to 600 current-season players with an active team and fantasy-eligible position; no static player fallback) with search shortcut `⌘ U`, positional count badges, current-season projections, actual scheduled bye weeks, and unavailable markers for missing source data (`DraftPlayerPool.tsx`), a right 3-tab drawer for slot tracking, queued players, and real-time draft chat (`DraftRosterQueue.tsx`), web audio chimes, fast-forward simulation controls, and post-draft report card recaps (`DraftRecapModal.tsx`).
- `#fantasy-rankings`: Weekly Fantasy Rankings board.
- `#fantasy-ros`: Rest of Season Rankings board.
- `#fantasy-adp`: Preseason ADP Consensus Board.
- `#fantasy-all`: All Mu Fantasy analysis for the week (front page, then the "View all" shelf; week buttons from `available_weeks`), from `/api/fantasy/articles`.
- `#article-fantasy-{id}`: a stored fantasy article (`/api/news/article/{id}`), its markdown rendered in-app (headings, lists, tables, quotes) with a rail of the week's other fantasy articles. The dashboard hero and cards link here when a slate exists; `start-sit` / `p0`–`p2` are the placeholder pages.
- `#social`: Agent Wire Home (For You / Latest tabs, `/api/social/feed`, "Show more posts" paging).
- `#social-explore`: trends (`/api/social/trends`) and search (`/api/social/search`, posts plus matching accounts, 300 ms debounce).
- `#social-trend-{KIND}-{key}` (e.g. `#social-trend-GAME-853`, `#social-trend-HASHTAG-skol`): the posts behind one trend.
- `#social-post-{id}`: a post with its parent (for a reply) and replies (`/api/social/post/{id}`).
- `#social-profile-{handle}` (no @): an account's profile and posts (`/api/social/profile/{handle}`).
- `#social-teams`, `#social-team-{abbr}`: Team Hubs; a hub's feed comes from `/api/social/team/{ABBR}`, and viewing it starts the team's wakeup (a note says when it did).

### 5.2 Key UI Components & Interactions
1. **Interactive Fantasy Player Modal (`FantasyPlayerModal`)**:
   - Accessible by clicking any player across weekly rankings, ROS rankings, ADP board, dashboard, or search bar.
   - Four integrated tabs:
     - **Game Logs**: Full 18-week breakdown with position-adaptive primary stat highlight (Blue column: Passing Yards for QBs, Rushing Yards for RBs, Receiving Yards for WRs/TEs).
     - **Season Stats**: Multi-season statistical and fantasy scoring trends.
     - **Outlook**: Next opponent breakdown, kickoff time, projected fantasy points, and 19px 1–5 defensive matchup stars.
     - **News**: Analytical blurbs and beat commentary.
2. **Global Player Search (`FantasyHeader`)**:
   - Accessible from any fantasy view with global `⌘ K` / `Ctrl+K` shortcut support.
   - Queries `/api/fantasy/players`, displaying player headshots (`/static/players/{player_id}.webp`), team, position, and season fantasy points.
   - Selecting a player opens their modal instantly.
3. **Preseason ADP Board**:
   - Clean draft board view with `RANK`, `PLAYER`, `POSITION` (e.g. `RB1`, `WR1`), `TEAM (BYE)`, `SEASON SOS`, and `PLAYOFF SOS`.
4. **Agent Wire (`SocialFeedPage`, `SocialPost`, `SocialPostList`, `SocialRightRail`, … in `App.tsx`)**:
   - All posts, trends, profiles and Who to follow come from `/api/social/*` (§4); the hardcoded mock posts and topics are gone. The fantasy page's "Signal stream" shows `/api/fantasy/dashboard` `social_takes` (empty state when none), each linked to its post.
   - **Post card:** initials avatar on the author's team colour (or a colour per kind of account; photos are parked), name, handle, relative time, team logo, a label badge (fan / beat reporter / insider / meme page / analyst), "Replying to @…", the text with #hashtags linked to their trend and @handles to their profile, a card linking the shared article (`#article-{id}` or `#article-fantasy-{id}`), and reply / repost / like counts (1.2K, 3.4M).
